- **s14 env lesson (BINDING while this mount era lasts): git unlink is EPERM on the mount** — every git write op leaves stale `*.lock` + `tmp_obj_*` in `.git`, and the NEXT op dies on "File exists" (s14 hit the wedge live). Recipe: before EACH git op, SAME-DIR rename stale locks — `for f in $(find .git -maxdepth 2 -name "*.lock"); do mv "$f" "${f%.lock}.stale$(date +%s%N)"; done` (cross-dir mv falls back to copy+unlink and leaves GHOSTS — burned s14 twice; same-dir rename is the one primitive that works, it's how git's own lock→target renames succeed). Run git with `-c core.logAllRefUpdates=false` (no HEAD.lock/reflog second-lock), ignore unlink warnings, and treat "update_ref failed" as MAYBE-LANDED — verify `git log` before retrying (two s14 probes landed despite errors, soft-reset off). Mac lane cleanup, no rush: `rm -f .git/*.stale* .git/objects/*.stale* .git/objects/*/tmp_obj_* && rm -rf .git/stale-locks-s14 && git gc` (junk is inert).

## Session collision protocol (BINDING — unchanged)
1. On start: read the `Last updated` line. `ACTIVE <ts>` < 3 h old → another lane is live: docs/review/probe only.
2. Set `ACTIVE <ISO>` + commit on start; clear it in the handoff commit. 3b. TOCTOU guard (added s34 after 13:05 race): after your lock commit, run `git log --oneline <your-parent>..HEAD` — if a FOREIGN lock commit landed between the CLEARED state you read and your lock, earlier lock wins: the later session reverts its own lock commit and exits. Before ANY merge to main, re-check for foreign commits since your lock (compare-and-swap); if found, discard your result and re-review.
3. Before rsync-back: `git log --oneline -3` on the mount; newer commits than your ~/gr baseline → STOP, 3-way merge, re-run suite.
4. rsync-back excludes `STATUS.md` and `specs/` (edit those ON the mount); also exclude `test-results/`, `playwright-report/`, `dist/`. Do NOT use `--delete` on rsync-back (review files live mount-side only); rebuild-direction (mount→gr) keeps `--delete`.

## How to resume (next session — in order)
1. Load `allow_cowork_file_delete` tool BEFORE any rm/git work (call it only when a delete actually fails).
2. Re-provision sandbox (VM home may be wiped — s4 found codex/auth/libs INTACT but ~/gr stale and git identity missing): `git config --global user.name/email`; `export PATH=~/.npm-global/bin:$PATH; codex login status` (expect "Logged in using ChatGPT"); `ls ~/locallibs/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu` (libXdamage); `ls ~/.cache/ms-playwright` (chromium-1228 — NEVER `npx playwright install`); Playwright runs need `export LD_LIBRARY_PATH=~/locallibs/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu`.
2b. **(s44) Compiled libXdamage stub** (proxy 403s ports.ubuntu.com; empty stub fails — chrome full-RELRO): write no-op defs for XDamageQueryExtension/XDamageCreate/XDamageDestroy/XDamageSubtract to /tmp/stub.c, then `gcc -shared -fPIC -Wl,-soname,libXdamage.so.1 -o ~/locallibs/usr/lib/aarch64-linux-gnu/libXdamage.so.1 /tmp/stub.c`. Raw playwright API scripts: `executablePath=/tmp/pw-browsers/chromium-1228/chrome-linux/chrome`. npm i FOREGROUND `timeout 40 npm i --prefer-offline --cache /tmp/npm-cache` (background gets reaped). Build in /tmp/gr-sNN (never reuse /tmp/gr).
3. Rebuild ~/gr: `rm -rf ~/gr/.git && rsync -a --delete --exclude node_modules --exclude .git --exclude worktrees --exclude test-results --exclude playwright-report --exclude dist "mnt/Gold Rush/" ~/gr/ && cd ~/gr && npm i --prefer-offline --cache /tmp/npm-cache && git init -qb main && git add -A && git commit -qm baseline`. (worktrees/ is 1.2GB of lane checkouts — NEVER rsync it.)
4. Verify baseline green (see verification lessons), then 07 prep / M2 spec work.

## Codex chunked-exec protocol (proven; sessions E/F took ~15 chunks each)
- Launch in `~/gr`: `timeout -k 1 40 codex exec --dangerously-bypass-approvals-and-sandbox -m gpt-5.5 -c model_reasoning_effort="medium" "$(cat ~/task.md)" < /dev/null >> ~/log 2>&1`; loop `codex exec resume <id> ...` until exit 0 (124 = keep resuming).
- Task file must include: interrupted+resumed warning, write-files-early, servers die, NEVER playwright install, LD_LIBRARY_PATH, no git commit, don't touch STATUS/specs/reviews/existing e2e, and "reply READY-FOR-GATES — supervisor runs gates".
- **s4 lesson — write-livelock remedy:** if chunks keep dying mid-write ("writing X now" with nothing on disk), switch resumes to `model_reasoning_effort="low"` + explicit "TWO apply_patch calls of ~60 lines each, first tool call immediately, ZERO prose". Un-stuck both E and F instantly. Medium reasoning for planning/integration chunks, low for mechanical writing.
- Per-file progress probes (`wc -l`, `grep -c`) after each chunk beat reading the log.

## Verification lessons (cumulative — s27 additions at top)
- **s50: cp/`install -D` onto EXISTING tracked mount files FAILS (unlink denied) and the shell keeps going — the merge commit then silently misses every modified file.** Overwrite in-place with `cat source > target` (no unlink path), `cmp` to verify, and grep `git status` staged list against the slice inventory before committing. New files are unaffected. Same mount law family as s40's broken `git checkout --`.
- **s27: `expect.poll`/`toPass` carry their OWN 5s default timeout — `test.setTimeout` does NOT feed them.** Any suite that "fixed" slowness with a test-timeout bump alone but polls past 5s is still broken (m2-03 was; audit new suites for this).
- **s27: global renderer counters (info.memory.\*) are not pure functions of the system under test** — boot lazies land for seconds and vary run-to-run (baselines 9 vs 28, same build/machine). Assert cycle-correlated growth (quiesce → warm → grace cycle → zero-growth cycles), never absolute equality across a live window; per-frame `calls` compares as resting floors (min over a window), never instantaneous samples (1-2 frame post-swap transients).
- **s27: sub-frame-gap visibility windows are protocol-invisible** — a 100ms fade completes inside one protocol round-trip at headless fps; only in-page rAF trackers armed BEFORE the trigger + monotonic counters (fadeWindow) observe them. s25's frame-gap law, applied to any transient overlay/flash assert.
- **s27: vite dev SPA-fallback poisons existence probes** — fetch of a missing asset path returns 200 text/html; require an image/\* content-type. (The icons gate caught this on itself first run.)
- **s27: stale `.last-run.json` can silently shrink a re-run's test set** — when a pass count looks short, re-run with full listing (`tail`), not `grep passed`.
- **s27 env: dead sessions' /tmp dirs persist as nobody:700** — never reuse `/tmp/gr`; build in `/tmp/gr-sNN`. Neighbor load >2 → per-project serial playwright runs; m1-01 mobile splits ["spawns Claim Jumpers|nospawn blocks" / "double restart|stress=120"].
- **s26: `net::ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES` = browser-level refusal class (trace `status: -1`)** — chromium refuses random resource loads under VM degradation; app null-caches (loadProcessedTexture caches null FOREVER) → permanent placeholder for that cell until page reload. Forensics recipe: unzip the trace, `0-trace.network` per-line JSON → request url + response.status (-1 = refused). A/B on pure HEAD before blaming a slice — s26's whole failure set reproduced on HEAD. Bounded page-reload retry heals it (only null-cached cells re-request; HTTP cache serves the rest).
- **s26: asset-dependent e2e must be lazy-load-aware** — cell textures load sequentially over seconds; never assert frameKeys from an eager fixed sample window after a direction flip. Pattern: in-page rAF collector with early exit on evidence-complete + generous deadline (vp-02 probe test is the reference).
- **s26 env (this VM era): HOME SHARES the 99% /sessions disk — `~/gr` won't fit; build in `/tmp/gr`** (root disk, 3.2G free). Bare `/tmp/<file>` writes are Permission-denied — put logs INSIDE /tmp/gr. `timeout 40 npx playwright ...` inside the 45s bash wall keeps died-call zombies from eating a run's verdict. captureScreenshot can fail outright on the degraded compositor (retry once; else defer shots to the healthy sweep).
- **s25: pin non-gated lanes to HEAD in ~/gr.** When another lane's output lands mid-gate, every rsync silently builds a COMBINED tree — after each mount→gr rsync, `git show HEAD:<file> > ~/gr/<file>` for all files belonging to lanes you are NOT gating, and re-run anything that executed after the leak window. Check `git status` against your triage inventory before EVERY commit — new modified files = a live lane.
- **s25 env recipe (degraded-VM hour + disk):** `TMPDIR=/tmp` + `--trace=off` on every playwright call (Chrome profile + traces on /sessions crash pages at 99% disk); `PLAYWRIGHT_BROWSERS_PATH=/tmp/pw-browsers` explicitly per call (the ~/.cache symlink VANISHED mid-session once); npm cache at `/tmp/npm-cache` survives wipes; `rm -rf ~/gr/test-results` between batches; rsync `--exclude worktrees` ALWAYS (1.2GB on the mount). At ~6fps a protocol round-trip spans ~2 frames: any "sampled N times" or two-evaluate consistency assert is a coin flip — atomic one-evaluate samples or poll-the-tracker first (m2-02/m2-06 modernized `40b674a`; A/B on pure HEAD before touching any canary, 4 A/Bs run this fire, all env).
- **s25: `| head` eats tsc's exit code** — `npx tsc 2>&1 | head -3; echo $?` reports head's status. Run `npx tsc; echo TSC-EXIT:$?` bare, or check output emptiness, and run it AFTER any e2e-file edit (Playwright transpiles without typechecking — a type-broken canary edit still "passes").
- **s23: diagnostics singletons need additive contracts once a slot gains a second writer** — two `GeneratedSpriteBatch` instances on one slot id zeroed the shared `renderedSprites` count last-writer-wins (thief batch, usually empty, always wrote last). Fix pattern: per-writer contribution tracking, adjust-by-delta. Suspect this family whenever a "count stays 0 while the thing is plainly alive" symptom appears after a pooled-renderer split.
- **s23: HUD-vs-state consistency asserts must be atomic (one in-page evaluate)** — sample-then-assert (`gold()` → `toContainText`) races live income at high timescale, and `toContainText`'s retry loop retries a STALE expected string. Chain overhead widened the gap enough to expose it (m2-02). Same family: assert-what-you-sampled, delta-from-event (m2-04 pathing fixed to delta-from-spawn this fire).
- **s23: when a spec SANCTIONS a surface change, its canaries' assumptions need re-examination at the gate** — vp-02's fallback test blocked only the sheets it knew about; 008's rotation cells legitimately intercepted the fallback. Amend the canary to preserve its INTENT (block more, still demand the last fallback layer), never weaken it. Document in-file + in the review.
- **s23 45s-wall updates:** m1-03 and m1-05 full files now exceed one bash call — m1-03 splits ["grace holds|wave banner|spawn accounting" / "alive cap|resetRun mid-wave"], m1-05 ["keyboard-build|registers kills|river placement" / "cost escalates|owner kills|harvest channel"]; m2-02 splits fine 3+["bank cap"]+["R rotates|four tappable"]; vp-02b runs per-test (8-direction and hysteresis ~19s each) and its shots test carries `test.setTimeout(60_000)`.
- **s10: git on the mount leaves STALE `.git/HEAD.lock`/`index.lock`** when the Cowork delete guard blocks git's own cleanup ("unable to unlink … Operation not permitted" warnings on an otherwise-successful commit; the NEXT commit then fails "cannot lock ref HEAD"). Recipe: load+call `allow_cowork_file_delete` at first delete failure, `rm -f .git/HEAD.lock .git/index.lock`, retry. Check for stale locks at session start. Same guard blocks `git checkout --` restores of binaries until enabled.
- **s10: renderer-memory gates must warm pool DEPTH, not just first use** — m1-02 texture assert failed 20→21 on clean HEAD on this slow VM: concurrent floats reach deeper float-text pool slots first-time DURING cycles (retained canvas upload; geoms stable). m1-05's warm-cycle pattern is now applied in m1-02 too (`0abbab2`). Suspect this pattern for any future "±1 texture/geometry" flake before blaming a slice.
- **s10: this VM's /tmp SURVIVED re-provision** (chromium-1228 intact; only ~ was wiped) — check `/tmp/pw-browsers` before re-downloading. Heredoc gotcha: don't use `EOF` as delimiter in a bash call that also pipes another heredoc/multiline — a silent no-op patch resulted; use distinct delimiters (PYEOF) and assert-verify with grep after patching.
- **s6 env variance:** VM home fully wiped (rebuild recipe worked; `.codex-auth/auth.json` on the mount is the auth seed); /sessions disk hit 100% (other tenants) → playwright browsers now live at `/tmp/pw-browsers` via `~/.cache/ms-playwright` symlink — RE-CHECK the symlink next session (/tmp wipes on VM restart); chromium fetched direct: `curl -C - https://cdn.playwright.dev/dbazure/download/playwright/builds/chromium/1228/chromium-linux-arm64.zip` (playwright install never fits the 45s wall). Background processes reaped ~60-90s (`setsid nohup` included): vite MUST start in the same bash call as its test batch — `fuser -k 5189/tcp; (npx vite --port 5189 --strictPort &) && sleep 3 && curl -s guard` then ONE batch <35s. This per-call kill+fresh-vite pattern also dodges s5's zombie-vite/stale-code hazard.
- **Headless fps floor is a fiction under load: s6 saw 6fps** (vs s4's 17-22) — ALL throughput/visibility asserts must be in-page rAF trackers, never protocol polling (m1-02 hardened `93ddbae`; m1-06 was already right).
- **Overlay `visible` lies:** hidden overlays are opacity-hidden → playwright counts them visible; `heroHp` is NOT a diagnostics key (`?? 100` guards made waits pass vacuously). Death waits: poll `state === 'dead'` or use the suite's `forceDeath`. Separation rings CAP contact DPS — huge point-blank packs form a donut and stall (~4 contacts/4s); overwhelm ≠ big pack, use forceDeath's repeated tight spawns.
- **Probe-your-probe:** two s6 misdiagnoses (hit-pause "perma-freeze", "death never happens") came from polling nonexistent keys / protocol sampling. Verify the observable exists (`JSON.stringify` drops undefined silently) before trusting a 0.

- **New-mechanic slices change sim semantics under OLD suites — always run the FULL regression, never just the new spec file.** m1-06's level-freeze deadlocked m1-01's death test (caught) and made m1-02's kill counts a mote-collection race (latent). Fix pattern: harness param family — `?nolevel` (XP math intact, no offer/freeze) joins `?nowaves/?nokill/?nospawn`.
- **Bolt-diffusion:** dumb bolts hit the first pool-ordered enemy on the flight line — in a clump, damage diffuses (probed: 7/7 hits, 56 dmg, zero kills across 6×28 HP). Kill-attribution asserts need SINGLE-enemy scenarios (all bolts concentrate → deterministic kill). Receding/approaching targets always get hit; crossers/clumps diffuse.
- **Playwright tracing (`retain-on-failure`) records during passing runs and shifts timing** — it re-rolls race coins. Never chase trace-on-vs-off as the bug; find the underlying nondeterminism (probe at volley/hit level with temporary guarded console.warn instrumentation — then REVERT it).
- **Renderer-memory leak gates need a warm cycle along the measured camera path** — first visibility lazily uploads frustum-culled geometry (+1 on a mere teleport). Pattern: run one full identical cycle before baselining (m1-05 reset test), plus `__GR_TEST__.warmVfx()` for the float-text pool (m1-01/02).
- **Sub-frame windows (volley-2 double bolt ≈ 1 headless frame) can't be seen by protocol polling** — install an in-page rAF max-tracker via evaluate and read it after.
- **aria/page snapshots list HIDDEN overlays** (death + upgrade overlays are permanently in DOM) — don't misread them as visible when debugging failures.
- Freeze/drift asserts: never compare against a pre-keypress sample (sim runs between sample and key landing — 0.45 s at ×3); assert within the frozen window and use the identity `Δ(nextWaveInSim) ≡ Δ(timeAlive)` for un-drift.
- Count summaries: `grep -E "[0-9]+ (passed|failed|skipped)"` — never tail. E2e SERIAL (`--workers=1`), `--project=desktop-chrome` (23→38 tests), per spec file, `-g` batches sized <35 s; ONE batch per bash call (two in one call breached the 45 s wall).
- `pw.reuse.config.ts` (port 5189) + guarded start: `(curl -s -o /dev/null http://127.0.0.1:5189/ || ((npx vite --port 5189 --strictPort >log 2>&1 &) && sleep 3))` — orphan vite sometimes survives between calls, sometimes not. Kill only via `fuser -k 5189/tcp`.
- Death tests must OVERWHELM; spawnPack lands ON the hero — a 4-pack vs an upgraded rig is a death coin flip, use 1-enemy packs for non-death asserts.
- Headless SwiftShader 17–22 fps; fps floor ≥12; draw calls are the real gate (≤200; now ~35 with 6 beacons @ stress=120). frameMs avg/p95 in diagnostics.
- `__GR_TEST__` surface: teleport, spawnPack, resetRun, warmVfx, grantGold, grantXp, setBuildMode, placeBeacon, state. Debug keys: T pack, X +50 XP (?debug only).
